        ### Constraints
        - MUST NOT touch solana_client.py / solana_provider.py — the rebroadcast loop, priority fees, status.err handling all stay.
- MUST NOT touch grants_cog. Grants keeps its own flow (test auto-confirmed by bot, recipient clicks PaymentConfirmView button). grants_cog.py:719 call to send_confirmation_request MUST remain functional.
- MUST preserve hardening invariants: per-user-per-day caps, canonical wallet swap defense, idempotency collision detection, WalletUpdateBlockedError, RLS comments, prompt-injection hardening (wallet lookups only from wallet_registry), null-recipient fail-closed in PaymentService.confirm_payment.
- MUST preserve cleanup invariants: PaymentActor/PRODUCER_FLOWS registry, tx_signature_history column, PaymentWorkerCog/PaymentUICog split, safe_delete_messages primitive.
- MUST preserve polish invariants: reconcile_with_chain, dynamic priority fees, /payment-resolve slash command, provider name migration, docs/payments.md, docs/runbook-payments.md, scripts/check_payment_invariants.py, structured tx_confirm_decision logging.
- Agent MUST NEVER accept LLM-sourced wallet addresses. initiate_batch_payment and upsert_wallet_for_user must reuse the existing 'pop and warn' guard pattern from execute_initiate_payment at tools.py:2501-2508 (for the batch), and upsert_wallet_for_user must take the wallet_address explicitly from a validated admin parameter (not from unrelated LLM context).
- verified_at MUST only be set after a real round-trip: on-chain test confirmation + recipient text acknowledgment. Neither alone is sufficient. upsert_wallet_for_user creates the row with verified_at=NULL.
- Admin DM approval gate fires ONLY for real (is_test=False) payments under producer='admin_chat'. NEVER for is_test=True. NEVER for producer='grants'.
- One admin DM per payment (not per batch). A batch of 16 → 16 DMs. Each DM carries its own AdminApprovalView with a unique custom_id.
- AdminApprovalView must be persistent (timeout=None) and re-registered on bot startup via cog_load for restart recovery. Pattern: match PaymentConfirmView registration in payment_ui_cog.py.
- PaymentConfirmView must NO LONGER fire for producer='admin_chat' real payments. Grants keeps using it. The gating should be at the call site in admin_chat_cog.py (remove/replace the send_confirmation_request call) — do NOT add a producer check inside send_confirmation_request because that would couple PaymentUICog to producer semantics.
- Existing admin_payment_intents schema: a SQL migration must add 'awaiting_test_receipt_confirmation' and 'awaiting_admin_approval' to the status CHECK constraint; migration must be idempotent (DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS / ADD CONSTRAINT) and ship with the code change.
- Identity router for approved-member messages: under the new routing, approved members' messages are no longer forwarded to the agent unless they are the admin. Document explicitly — this is an intentional scoping reduction per the task spec. MEMBER_SYSTEM_PROMPT is NOT deleted (dead code left in place).
- Keyword lists for confirmation/negative/ambiguous MUST be hardcoded in code (no LLM call). Case-insensitive match, whole-word boundary preferred, include emoji variants ('👍'). Ambiguous = neither positive nor negative.
- 24h timeout: new background task (add to AdminChatCog or PaymentWorkerCog) that scans admin_payment_intents with status=awaiting_test_receipt_confirmation AND updated_at < now-24h, transitions to manual_review, and DMs admin. Interval ≥ 5 minutes to avoid DB hammering.
- Batch tool atomicity: prefer Supabase .insert([list]) (PostgREST atomic per-request) for create_admin_payment_intents_batch. If any row fails validation BEFORE insert, reject the whole batch with zero DB writes. If the DB rejects the multi-row insert, the whole request fails atomically.
- Test churn expected: test_admin_payments.py (1350 lines) will see broad rewrites. New tests MUST NOT mock _classify_payment_reply (it's no longer called from the state machine path). They SHOULD continue to assert WalletUpdateBlockedError paths.
- DO NOT add a cog-layer rejection of agent replies that make state claims without query-tool calls. Per the task: system-prompt guidance only, no enforcement.
- DO NOT start nested megaplan plans or invoke the megaplan CLI from within implementation — treat megaplan mentions as context only.

        ### Suggested Approach
        Land this in phased, reviewable chunks that keep tests green at every step.

PHASE 1 — Schema + state enum (smallest diff, lowest risk):
(a) SQL migration: extend admin_payment_intents status CHECK to add 'awaiting_test_receipt_confirmation' and 'awaiting_admin_approval'. Idempotent DROP/ADD CONSTRAINT pattern. Update sql/admin_payment_intents.sql + any migration runner.
(b) db_handler helpers: add create_admin_payment_intents_batch (Supabase .insert([list])), a list_intents_by_status helper, a list_stale_test_receipt_intents(cutoff) for the 24h sweep, and an ambiguous-reply counter update path.

PHASE 2 — Identity router (on_message rewrite):
Replace the current on_message + _check_pending_payment_reply branching with a single switch:
 1. If author.id == ADMIN_USER_ID → existing agent path (unchanged except new tools added in phase 4).
 2. Else if db_handler.get_active_intent_for_recipient returns a row → dispatch to a new _handle_pending_recipient_message(intent, message) that routes by intent.status (awaiting_wallet, awaiting_test_receipt_confirmation). No LLM calls.
 3. Else → return without handling (fallthrough).
Delete the Claude-based _classify_payment_reply code path from the message-handler flow (keep the function around for the phase-2 diff small; delete in a later cleanup commit if truly unused).
Add deterministic helpers:
 - `_parse_wallet_from_text(content) -> str | None` reusing is_valid_solana_address.
 - `_classify_confirmation(content) -> Literal['positive','negative','ambiguous']` with hardcoded keyword lists from the task spec (whole-word, case-insensitive, emoji-aware).

PHASE 3 — Flow rewiring (test-receipt + admin-DM gate):
(a) Modify handle_payment_result's test-success branch: instead of creating the final payment inline, transition intent to awaiting_test_receipt_confirmation, post the 'please check your wallet and reply confirmed' prompt in the thread, store the prompt_message_id.
(b) When _handle_pending_recipient_message gets a positive confirmation in awaiting_test_receipt_confirmation: call db_handler.mark_wallet_verified, create the real payment via payment_service.request_payment (is_test=False), transition intent to awaiting_admin_approval, and fire the admin DM.
(c) Auto-skip path: in execute_initiate_payment (and execute_initiate_batch_payment), when a verified wallet exists, create the real payment directly and go straight to awaiting_admin_approval — skip _start_admin_payment_flow (which is test-only). This is a branch on wallet_record.verified_at at intent-creation time, not inside _start_admin_payment_flow.
(d) Admin DM: new helper `_send_admin_approval_dm(payment)` in PaymentUICog that DMs ADMIN_USER_ID with amount/recipient/redacted-wallet/intent_id/jump-link and attaches a new AdminApprovalView keyed by payment_id. On discord.Forbidden → log ERROR, leave intent in awaiting_admin_approval (fail-closed, no public fallback).
(e) New AdminApprovalView class in payment_ui_cog.py, pattern-copied from PaymentConfirmView. Button callback verifies interaction.user.id == ADMIN_USER_ID, calls payment_service.confirm_payment, edits the DM to ✅, follows up with 'payment confirmed, sending', posts 'Payment sent to @user' in the intent's thread, and deletes stale flow messages via safe_delete_messages.
(f) Persistence: PaymentUICog.cog_load gains a _register_pending_admin_approval_views pass that queries admin_payment_intents WHERE status='awaiting_admin_approval' and bot.add_view(AdminApprovalView(...)) for each.
(g) Remove the admin_chat_cog.py:393 send_confirmation_request call. Grants' call at grants_cog.py:719 stays untouched.

PHASE 4 — New agent tools:
(a) Three read-only query tools (query_payment_state, query_wallet_state, list_recent_payments): thin wrappers over existing db_handler helpers with wallet redaction applied via the existing redact_wallet import. Add to TOOLS list in tools.py, dispatch in execute_tool, and list in SYSTEM_PROMPT.
(b) initiate_batch_payment: takes payments=[{recipient_user_id, amount_sol, reason?}, ...]. Per-payment LLM-wallet-injection guard (pop wallet_address/recipient_wallet and warn, same as current execute_initiate_payment). Validate every element first (guild_id, channel_id, amount > 0, no active duplicate for recipient), build the payload list, call db_handler.create_admin_payment_intents_batch. On success, iterate the returned rows and fan out through the same per-payment mechanics as initiate_payment (auto-skip branch for verified wallets OR _start_admin_payment_flow for unverified). Each verified path fires its own admin DM — so a 16-payment batch produces 16 DMs.
(c) upsert_wallet_for_user: takes (user_id, wallet_address, chain='solana', reason?). Admin-only check via a requester context flag. Validate wallet_address via is_valid_solana_address. Call db_handler.upsert_wallet (propagating WalletUpdateBlockedError to the admin reply). Never sets verified_at. On success, DM the admin: 'wallet for @user set to {redacted}, will be verified on next payment'.
(d) SYSTEM_PROMPT addition: a new 'State questions → query first' section instructing the agent to call query_payment_state / query_wallet_state / list_recent_payments before claiming state. Soft guidance, not enforcement. Add the five new tool lines to the 'Doing things' section of the prompt (3 query + 1 batch + 1 upsert).

PHASE 5 — 24h timeout sweep:
New background task (recommend AdminChatCog, since it owns intent lifecycle). @tasks.loop(minutes=15) scans admin_payment_intents WHERE status='awaiting_test_receipt_confirmation' AND updated_at < now()-interval '24 hours', transitions each to manual_review, and DMs admin with full context (intent_id, recipient, tx_signature, wallet).

PHASE 6 — Tests:
Rewrite test_admin_payments.py broadly: new router tests, state-machine tests, keyword parser tests (parametrized), batch atomicity test, upsert admin-check test, auto-skip test, AdminApprovalView persistence + click test, admin DM fail-closed test, gated PaymentConfirmView test, 24h timeout test. Keep pass_to_pass tests (payment state machine, race, reconcile, authorization, solana client, safe delete, invariants, tx signature history) untouched; verify they stay green.

PHASE 7 — Docs:
Append to docs/payments.md and docs/runbook-payments.md: identity routing overview, new states, admin DM approval gate, auto-skip rule, batch tool semantics, upsert tool semantics, and the scoping-reduction note for approved members.

Flagged risks / open questions the plan step should resolve:
 - Batch atomicity when Supabase multi-insert partially fails: confirm PostgREST behavior (should be atomic per-request). If not, fall back to per-row inserts inside a new SQL RPC function wrapped in a txn.
 - The prompt_message_id column already exists on admin_payment_intents but currently tracks only one prompt; the new flow posts TWO prompts (wallet request + 'please check and reply confirmed'). Consider adding a second column (e.g., receipt_prompt_message_id) or reusing prompt_message_id by overwriting — prefer reuse with a comment, since only the latest is needed for safe-delete.
 - The ambiguous-reply counter needs a new integer column (e.g., ambiguous_reply_count default 0) on admin_payment_intents, or store it in a JSONB metadata column if one exists. Verify whether metadata exists; if not, add the integer column in the same migration.
 - AdminApprovalView custom_id collision: use f'payment_admin_approve:{payment_id}' — must not collide with the existing f'payment_confirm:{payment_id}' (they don't, but grep to confirm).

        The engineering brief above was produced by analyzing the codebase. Use it as a strong starting point, but the suggested approach is a hypothesis — verify it's the best fix, not just a valid one.

        Idea:
Identity-based routing for admin_chat with pending-recipient state machine, batch payment creation, admin DM approval gate, auto-skip test for verified wallets, and wallet upsert tooling.

ARCHITECTURAL CHANGE — identity-based router replaces content-based message classification. After this megaplan lands, admin_chat message handling routes entirely by WHO the author is, not by WHAT they said:

1. If author is ADMIN_USER_ID → full agent path (Claude with all tools including new ones). The agent has access to query tools (new, read-only) and action tools (new initiate_payment batch variant, new upsert_wallet_for_user). System prompt softly guides the agent to use query tools for state questions (not strict enforcement — the admin can correct hallucinations; money cannot move without an explicit tool call so the safety is structural).

2. If author has an active non-terminal row in admin_payment_intents (a pending payment recipient) → pure state machine. No LLM in the loop. The state machine reads the message text and routes based on the intent's current state. Two states matter:
   - awaiting_wallet: parse message for a Solana-address-shaped string (base58, 32-44 chars, proper byte length). If found, register the wallet (verified_at=NULL), trigger test payment. If not found, ignore silently (no spamming).
   - awaiting_test_receipt_confirmation (NEW STATE): parse message for confirmation keywords ('confirmed', 'received', 'got it', 'yes', 'yep', 'confirm', '👍'), negative keywords ('no', 'didnt', 'not received', 'missing', 'nothing'), or ambiguous. On confirmation → mark wallet_registry.verified_at=now, transition intent to queue the real payment. On negative → tag admin in thread with full context (tx signature, recipient wallet, intent id). On ambiguous → ignore the first ambiguous message; tag admin on the second.

3. Otherwise → fall through (the admin_chat cog does nothing; other cogs like grants/gating/logging handle what they handle).

RECIPIENT-FACING FLOW CHANGES:

- First-ever payment to a new wallet: recipient posts wallet (1 text interaction) → bot sends 0.002 SOL test → bot posts 'Please check your wallet and reply confirmed when you see it' → recipient replies with confirmation text (1 text interaction) → state machine marks wallet verified → real payment auto-queues → admin DM approval gate fires → admin clicks → real payment sends. RECIPIENT TOTAL: 2 text interactions.
- Subsequent payments to a now-verified wallet: skip test entirely. Bot creates real payment → admin DM approval → admin clicks → send. RECIPIENT TOTAL: 0 interactions.
- Drop the existing PaymentConfirmView button for admin_chat real payments. The admin DM is now the sole real-payment gate. PaymentUICog.send_confirmation_request should no longer fire for producer='admin_chat' real payments. It still fires for grants (grants is unaffected by this megaplan; keeps its own flow).

NEW TOOLS FOR THE ADMIN AGENT:

A) query_payment_state(payment_id=None, user_id=None) → returns canonical DB state for a payment or all recent payments for a user. Read-only.
B) query_wallet_state(user_id) → returns wallet_registry rows for a user (address, verified_at, created_at). Read-only.
C) list_recent_payments(producer=None, user_id=None, limit=20) → returns recent payment_requests rows with redacted wallets. Read-only.
D) initiate_batch_payment(payments=[{recipient_user_id, amount_sol, reason?}, ...]) → creates N admin_payment_intents atomically in a single DB transaction. Each flows through the normal per-payment mechanical pipeline: if wallet unverified, runs the test → receipt confirmation cycle; once verified, real payment auto-queues; admin DM approval gate fires individually PER payment (one DM per payment, not per batch). If any intent creation fails, roll back all.
E) upsert_wallet_for_user(user_id, wallet_address, chain='solana', reason?) → admin-initiated wallet registration. Creates or updates wallet_registry with verified_at=NULL (verification still requires a test payment round-trip). On success, DMs the admin with a confirmation receipt ('wallet for @user set to 8ThP...xyz1, will be verified on next payment'). Does NOT mark verified_at.

ADMIN DM APPROVAL GATE:

- Fires ONLY for real (is_test=False) payments under producer='admin_chat'. Grants is unaffected.
- One DM per payment (even for batches — 16 payments = 16 DMs). The admin clicks Confirm on each.
- DM message format: clear amount, recipient, wallet (redacted), intent_id, jump link to original thread.
- On admin click: edit original DM to '✅ approved', send follow-up DM 'payment confirmed, sending', post reply in the intent's thread 'Payment sent to @user', delete stale flow messages in the thread via the existing safe_delete_messages primitive.
- New AdminApprovalView persistent view class, registered on bot startup for recovery across restarts.
- If admin DM delivery fails (discord.Forbidden, rate limit) → fail-closed, payment stays in awaiting_admin_approval, log at ERROR level. Do not auto-fall-back to public channel (assumption: DM delivery is reliable in practice; failure is rare enough to be a manual-ops event).

AUTO-SKIP TEST FOR VERIFIED WALLETS:

- When the admin initiates a payment via initiate_payment or initiate_batch_payment, and the recipient has a wallet_registry row with verified_at IS NOT NULL, skip the test payment creation entirely.
- Go straight to creating the real payment row, auto-queue, trigger admin DM approval.
- First-time and verified paths share the admin DM approval gate.

HALLUCINATION FIX (softer than originally proposed):

- Add the three query tools (query_payment_state, query_wallet_state, list_recent_payments) to the agent's tool palette.
- Update the admin agent's system prompt with a new section: 'For questions about the state of payments or wallets, call query_payment_state / query_wallet_state / list_recent_payments FIRST. Do not claim state from memory or channel context.'
- This is guidance, not enforcement. The agent CAN still hallucinate, but the operator (the admin) is the only audience, can correct, and money cannot move without an explicit tool call (which is visible and auditable).
- Do NOT add a cog-layer rejection of replies that make state claims without query tool calls — the user specifically said not to require tool calls.

WHAT THIS MEGAPLAN IS NOT TOUCHING (preserve existing invariants):

- solana_client.py / solana_provider.py — the rebroadcast loop, priority fees, status.err check, RPC unreachable handling all stay.
- Hardening pass inventory: per-user-per-day caps, canonical wallet swap defense, idempotency collision detection, WalletUpdateBlockedError, RLS comment, prompt-injection hardening in admin_chat/tools.py (wallet lookups only from wallet_registry), null-recipient fail-closed in PaymentService.confirm_payment.
- Cleanup inventory: PaymentActor/PRODUCER_FLOWS registry, tx_signature_history column, PaymentWorkerCog + PaymentUICog split, safe_delete_messages primitive.
- Polish inventory: reconcile_with_chain, dynamic priority fees, /payment-resolve slash command, provider name migration, docs/payments.md, docs/runbook-payments.md, scripts/check_payment_invariants.py, structured tx_confirm_decision logging.
- Grants cog — completely unaffected. Grants uses its own thread-scoped listener, has its own flow (test auto-confirmed by bot, recipient clicks button for real payment), and this megaplan does not touch it.
- The 'approved members can chat with bot' feature — the _can_user_message_bot path in admin_chat_cog. Under identity-based routing, approved-member messages are no longer forwarded to the agent unless the member is ALSO the ADMIN_USER_ID. This is an intentional scoping decision — the admin is the sole human who can conversationally interact with the agent. Document this in the plan explicitly so no one is surprised.

SUCCESS CRITERIA:

- Identity router correctly classifies admin, pending recipient, and fallthrough based solely on author identity.
- Pending recipient state machine handles awaiting_wallet and awaiting_test_receipt_confirmation deterministically with no LLM calls.
- Test-receipt confirmation flow: recipient's text acknowledgment is the sole gate for marking wallet_registry.verified_at (not on-chain confirmation alone).
- Negative / ambiguous recipient responses escalate to the admin via thread tag, not silent failure.
- 24-hour timeout on awaiting_test_receipt_confirmation → escalate to manual_review + admin DM.
- New tools (query_*, initiate_batch_payment, upsert_wallet_for_user) are admin-only and correctly scoped.
- initiate_batch_payment creates all intents atomically or none (DB transaction).
- Auto-skip test path correctly bypasses the test phase for already-verified wallets.
- Admin DM approval gate fires exactly once per real admin_chat payment, never for tests, never for grants.
- AdminApprovalView is persistent-safe across bot restarts.
- PaymentConfirmView button no longer fires for admin_chat real payments (grants-only now).
- No regression in existing hardening/cleanup/polish invariants — all existing tests pass.
- New tests cover: identity router branches, state machine state transitions, wallet regex parsing, confirmation keyword matching, batch tool atomicity, upsert_wallet_for_user admin check, auto-skip verified path, admin DM approval view persistence and click handling.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Identity-Based Admin Chat Routing + Admin-DM Approval Gate (Revision 4)

## Overview
Refactor `admin_chat` from content-based classification to strict identity-based routing (admin → agent, active-intent recipient → deterministic state machine, else → fallthrough). Gate recipient `verified_at` on a text-ack of the on-chain test. Replace `PaymentConfirmView` with a DM-only `AdminApprovalView` for `producer='admin_chat'` NEW-flow real payments, authorized via the already-existing `PaymentActorKind.ADMIN_DM`. Add 5 new agent tools plus `resolve_admin_intent` as the minimum operator surface. Auto-skip the test for verified wallets. Add a 24h timeout sweep. Grants + all hardening/cleanup/polish invariants untouched.

**Revision 4 applies three surgical fixes on top of Revision 3's resolved work**, addressing the iteration-3 gate flags:
1. **No more `awaiting_wallet` placeholder for verified-wallet fast path.** Ordering becomes: (a) duplicate check, (b) create `payment_requests` row first, (c) INSERT the intent atomically with BOTH `status='awaiting_admin_approval'` AND `final_payment_id` set in the SAME insert (not a later update), (d) fire admin DM. No placeholder state ever exists. Closes FLAG-013, correctness-1, callers-1, issue_hints-2, all_locations-2.
2. **Startup orphan-payment reconciliation sweep** runs BEFORE `_register_pending_confirmation_views`. It walks every `pending_confirmation` `producer='admin_chat'` `is_test=False` payment, and based on whether the intent referenced by `payment.metadata.intent_id` exists and is wired, either (i) recovers it by completing the intent update + firing the admin DM (test-receipt crash window), or (ii) cancels the orphan payment via the existing `cancel_payment` primitive (auto-skip crash window). `PaymentConfirmView` is never the crash fallback for new-flow admin_chat real payments — the sweep handles both windows cleanly. Closes FLAG-012, issue_hints-1, scope-1.
3. **`resolve_admin_intent` cascades cancellation to linked `payment_requests`.** The executor fetches the intent, and for each of `test_payment_id` and `final_payment_id` that is non-null and in a cancellable status (`pending_confirmation` / `queued`), calls the existing `db_handler.cancel_payment(payment_id, guild_id)` (mirroring `admin_chat_cog.py:616-620`). Only after the cascade does it update the intent row to `cancelled`. Closes FLAG-014, correctness-2, scope-2, all_locations-1, callers-2.

Everything outside these three surgical edits carries over from Revision 3 unchanged: schema (minus the now-deleted awaiting_wallet placeholder rationale), DB helpers, producer_flows authorization, state-aware restart filter (still needed for LEGACY `awaiting_confirmation` rows only), identity router, deterministic parsers, state-machine dispatch, test-receipt handlers, AdminApprovalView, new agent tools, ADMIN_ONLY_TOOLS / _ADMIN_IDENTITY_INJECTED_TOOLS extensions, 24h timeout sweep, tests.

Evidence carried from earlier revisions (unchanged):
- `PaymentActorKind.ADMIN_DM` at `payment_service.py:30`; `_authorize_actor` at `:539-546`.
- `PRODUCER_FLOWS['admin_chat'].real_confirmed_by` at `producer_flows.py:21-29`.
- `PaymentUICog._register_pending_confirmation_views` at `payment_ui_cog.py:110-130`.
- `admin_chat_cog.on_message` at `:790-817` — the `_is_directed_at_bot` gate drops for ADMIN_USER_ID.
- `ADMIN_ONLY_TOOLS` at `tools.py:908-936`; exhaustive assert at `:938-940`.
- `agent.py:431-441` — `admin_user_id` injection.
- `confirm_payment` at `payment_service.py:550-581` — transitions `pending_confirmation → queued`.
- `handle_payment_result` at `admin_chat_cog.py:416-440` — sole source of the "Payment sent." channel post.
- **`db_handler.cancel_payment(payment_id, guild_id)` at `db_handler.py:2402-2420`** — reusable primitive for cascade cancellation; used today by `admin_chat_cog.py:616-620`.
- **`create_admin_payment_intent` at `db_handler.py:1805-1819`** accepts `status` and `final_payment_id` in the initial payload — a single atomic insert CAN set both fields (verified by reading the method signature which just passes the record dict to Supabase `.insert`).
- **`sql/admin_payment_intents.sql:47-49`** — unique partial index on `(guild_id, channel_id, recipient_user_id) WHERE status NOT IN (completed, failed, cancelled)`. Inserting a second intent in `awaiting_admin_approval` for the same recipient while the first is still active will fail the constraint → DB-level atomicity guard.

Scope boundaries (unchanged): do NOT touch `solana_client.py`/`solana_provider.py`, `grants_cog.py`, `PaymentService` rebroadcast/fee logic, hardening/cleanup/polish invariants. `MEMBER_SYSTEM_PROMPT`, `_can_user_message_bot`, `_is_directed_at_bot` stay as dead code. `has_active_payment_or_intent` at `db_handler.py:1575-1601` unchanged. `manual_review` stays blocking.

## Phase 1: Schema + DB Helpers

### Step 1: Extend `admin_payment_intents` schema (`sql/admin_payment_intents.sql`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** idempotent `ALTER TABLE ... DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS ... ADD CONSTRAINT ...` extending the status CHECK to include `awaiting_test_receipt_confirmation`, `awaiting_admin_approval`, and `manual_review`, alongside existing statuses and `awaiting_confirmation` (kept for legacy in-flight rows).
2. **Add** `ambiguous_reply_count integer not null default 0` via `add column if not exists`.
3. **Add** `receipt_prompt_message_id bigint` via `add column if not exists`.
4. **DO NOT modify the unique partial index.** `manual_review` stays in the blocking set. The existing exclusion `('completed','failed','cancelled')` is correct.

### Step 2: DB helpers (`src/common/db_handler.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** `create_admin_payment_intents_batch(records: List[Dict], guild_id: int) -> Optional[List[Dict]]` — single Supabase `.insert([...])`. All-or-none.
2. **Add** `list_intents_by_status(guild_id: int, status: str) -> List[Dict]` for persistent-view registration.
3. **Add** `list_stale_test_receipt_intents(cutoff_iso: str) -> List[Dict]` for the 24h sweep.
4. **Add** `increment_intent_ambiguous_reply_count(intent_id: str, guild_id: int) -> Optional[int]`.
5. **Add** `get_pending_confirmation_admin_chat_intents_by_payment(payment_ids: List[str]) -> Dict[str, Optional[Dict]]` — returns `{payment_id: intent_row_or_None}` where the intent row is fetched by `metadata.intent_id` on the payment and filtered to producer='admin_chat'. Used by both the state-aware restart filter (Step 4) and the new orphan sweep (Step 18).
6. **Add** `find_admin_chat_intent_by_payment_id(payment_id: str) -> Optional[Dict]` — helper for the orphan sweep; looks up an intent by `final_payment_id == payment_id` or `test_payment_id == payment_id`. Used when `payment.metadata.intent_id` is missing or stale.
7. **Do NOT** modify `get_active_intent_for_recipient`, `list_active_intents`, or `has_active_payment_or_intent`.

## Phase 2: Payment Policy + Restart Recovery Filter (Interlocked with Phase 4 + 6)

### Step 3: Authorize `ADMIN_DM` for admin_chat real payments (`src/features/payments/producer_flows.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `PaymentActorKind.ADMIN_DM` to `admin_chat.real_confirmed_by`. Resulting set: `{RECIPIENT_CLICK, RECIPIENT_MESSAGE, ADMIN_DM}`. RECIPIENT_* stay as safety-belt for legacy in-flight rows.
2. **Do NOT** add a new enum value; reuse `ADMIN_DM`.
3. **Do NOT** touch grants.

### Step 4: State-aware restart-recovery filter for `PaymentConfirmView` (`src/features/payments/payment_ui_cog.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Modify** `_register_pending_confirmation_views` at `:113-130`. The filter runs AFTER the new orphan sweep (Step 18), so by the time it executes, every `pending_confirmation` admin_chat real payment either has a wired intent in `awaiting_admin_approval` OR has been cancelled. Rule:
   ```python
   for payment in pending:
       payment_id = payment.get('payment_id')
       if not payment_id:
           continue
       if payment.get('producer') == 'admin_chat' and not payment.get('is_test'):
           intent = intent_by_payment.get(payment_id)
           intent_status = (intent or {}).get('status')
           # NEW-flow: intent is awaiting_admin_approval → skip; AdminApprovalView handles it.
           if intent_status == 'awaiting_admin_approval':
               continue
           # LEGACY in-flight: intent status is awaiting_confirmation → keep PaymentConfirmView
           # so pre-deploy rows survive restart.
           # Anything else (orphan sweep would have cancelled) → skip defensively.
           if intent_status != 'awaiting_confirmation':
               continue
       self.bot.add_view(PaymentConfirmView(self, payment_id))
   ```
2. **Note:** `PaymentConfirmView` is NEVER re-registered for a NEW-flow admin_chat real payment. The only admin_chat case it handles is a legacy `awaiting_confirmation` row. This closes the critique that the "crash fallback" path contradicts the brief — crash recovery is now handled by Step 18, not by reverting to the recipient gate.
3. **Grants:** unaffected.

## Phase 3: Parsers + Identity Router

### Step 5: Deterministic parsers (`src/features/admin_chat/admin_chat_cog.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `_parse_wallet_from_text(content)` with tolerant delimiter regex `[\s`<>\"'(),\[\]{}*_~|]+`, trim trailing punctuation, validate each candidate via `is_valid_solana_address`.
2. **Add** `_classify_confirmation(content) -> Literal['positive','negative','ambiguous']` — hardcoded positive/negative keyword sets, case-insensitive, whole-word for single tokens, emoji substring for `👍`, multi-word phrases via bounded substring.
3. **Unit tests.**

### Step 6: Identity router (`src/features/admin_chat/admin_chat_cog.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Rewrite** `on_message` as a strict three-way switch on `message.author.id` (no `_is_directed_at_bot` gate for admin).
2. **Extract** `_handle_admin_message(message)` without the content gate and without the `_can_user_message_bot` branch. Preserve abort handling, busy queue, rate-limit exemption, channel context.
3. **Delete** the `_check_pending_payment_reply` call from `on_message`.
4. **Document** dead code (`_can_user_message_bot`, `_is_directed_at_bot`, `MEMBER_SYSTEM_PROMPT`).

### Step 7: State-machine dispatch (`src/features/admin_chat/admin_chat_cog.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** `_handle_pending_recipient_message(message, intent)` dispatching on `intent['status']`:
   - `awaiting_wallet` → `_parse_wallet_from_text`. If found → `_handle_wallet_received`. Silent otherwise.
   - `awaiting_test_receipt_confirmation` → `_classify_confirmation` → dispatch to `_handle_test_receipt_*` (Step 9).
   - All other non-terminal states (`awaiting_test`, `awaiting_admin_approval`, `manual_review`, `awaiting_confirmation`, `confirmed`) → silent return. Because `awaiting_admin_approval` never appears as a "placeholder" state (Rev-4 fix #1), a recipient posting an address-shaped message will never incorrectly reopen the test path.
2. **Reuse** `_processing_intents` dedupe set.
3. **Unit test invariant:** Anthropic client mock raises on any call.

## Phase 4: Flow Rewiring + Admin Approval Gate

### Step 8: Rewire `handle_payment_result` test-success branch (`src/features/admin_chat/admin_chat_cog.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Replace** the test-success branch at `:332-414`. On `is_test=True + status=='confirmed'`:
   - Transition intent to `awaiting_test_receipt_confirmation`.
   - Post the receipt prompt; store id in `receipt_prompt_message_id` (new column), do NOT overwrite `prompt_message_id`.
   - DO NOT create the real payment here.
   - DO NOT call `send_confirmation_request`. Delete the call at `:393`.
2. **Non-confirmed test status:** unchanged.

### Step 9: Test-receipt handlers + two gate helpers — no placeholder states (`src/features/admin_chat/admin_chat_cog.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** `_gate_existing_intent(channel, intent, wallet_record, amount_sol) -> Optional[Dict]` — used by the test-receipt positive handler (Step 9.3) where the intent ALREADY exists in `awaiting_test_receipt_confirmation`. Ordering:
   ```
   A. payment = payment_service.request_payment(is_test=False, producer='admin_chat',
          recipient_wallet=wallet_record['wallet_address'],
          metadata={'intent_id': intent['intent_id']},
          amount_token=amount_sol, ...) → pending_confirmation row.
   B. In ONE update call: update_admin_payment_intent(intent_id,
          {'status': 'awaiting_admin_approval', 'final_payment_id': payment['payment_id']}, guild_id).
   C. bot.payment_ui_cog._send_admin_approval_dm(payment).
   ```
   If the process dies between A and B: Step 18's orphan sweep on restart finds the `pending_confirmation` payment with `metadata.intent_id` pointing at an intent whose `final_payment_id != payment_id`. The sweep completes step B (update the intent) and fires the admin DM. Recovery is deterministic and does NOT reintroduce `PaymentConfirmView`.
2. **Add** `_gate_fresh_intent_atomic(channel, guild_id, recipient_user_id, amount_sol, source_channel_id, wallet_record, admin_user_id, reason, producer_ref) -> Optional[Dict]` — used by the auto-skip verified-wallet paths (Steps 11, 13) where NO intent exists yet. Ordering:
   ```
   A. Duplicate check: db_handler.get_active_intent_for_recipient(guild_id, source_channel_id,
          recipient_user_id). If an active intent exists → abort with duplicate error.
   B. payment = payment_service.request_payment(is_test=False, producer='admin_chat',
          recipient_wallet=wallet_record['wallet_address'],
          metadata={'intent_id': <reserved uuid>},  # generated client-side
          amount_token=amount_sol, ...) → pending_confirmation row.
       The reserved intent_id uuid is generated via uuid4() and stashed for step C.
   C. INSERT the intent row in a SINGLE atomic write:
          db_handler.create_admin_payment_intent({
              'intent_id': <reserved uuid>,
              'guild_id': guild_id,
              'channel_id': source_channel_id,
              'admin_user_id': admin_user_id,
              'recipient_user_id': recipient_user_id,
              'wallet_id': wallet_record['wallet_id'],
              'requested_amount_sol': amount_sol,
              'producer_ref': producer_ref,
              'reason': reason,
              'status': 'awaiting_admin_approval',   # NEVER a placeholder
              'final_payment_id': payment['payment_id'],
          }, guild_id=guild_id)
       The DB unique partial index on (guild_id, channel_id, recipient_user_id) WHERE status NOT IN (terminal) provides the atomicity guarantee for concurrent rapid-fire inits.
   D. bot.payment_ui_cog._send_admin_approval_dm(payment).
   ```
   If the process dies between B and C: orphan payment exists with no intent referencing it. Step 18's orphan sweep finds it via `metadata.intent_id` lookup returning `None` and cancels the orphan via `cancel_payment`. The admin gets nothing (safe — fail-closed), and the admin can simply re-run `initiate_payment` to create a fresh flow. No placeholder state, no `PaymentConfirmView` fallback.
3. **Add** `_handle_test_receipt_positive(message, intent)`:
   - `wallet_record = db_handler.get_wallet_by_id(intent['wallet_id'], guild_id=intent['guild_id'])`.
   - `db_handler.mark_wallet_verified(intent['wallet_id'], guild_id=intent['guild_id'])` — sole `verified_at` write path.
   - `_gate_existing_intent(channel, intent, wallet_record, intent['requested_amount_sol'])`.
   - Post thread ack.
4. **Add** `_handle_test_receipt_negative(message, intent)`:
   - Fetch test payment for `tx_signature` + wallet.
   - Post admin-tagged message in the source channel.
   - Transition intent to `manual_review`.
   - DM admin via `_notify_intent_admin`.
5. **Add** `_handle_test_receipt_ambiguous(message, intent)`:
   - `count = increment_intent_ambiguous_reply_count(...)`.
   - `count == 1` → silent. `count >= 2` → escalate via negative handler.

### Step 10: `AdminApprovalView` + admin DM helper (`src/features/payments/payment_ui_cog.py`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Add** `AdminApprovalView(discord.ui.View)` with `timeout=None`, single `Approve Payment` button at `custom_id=f"payment_admin_approve:{payment_id}"`. Callback:
   - Defer ephemeral.
   - Defense-in-depth admin check vs `ADMIN_USER_ID` env.
   - `confirm_payment(payment_id, guild_id=payment['guild_id'], actor=PaymentActor(PaymentActorKind.ADMIN_DM, interaction.user.id))`.
   - On `queued`: edit DM to "✅ approved — queued for sending", disable button; follow-up DM; channel post "payment has been approved by the admin and queued for sending"; `safe_delete_messages` with BOTH `prompt_message_id` and `receipt_prompt_message_id`.
   - **Do NOT** post "Payment sent" — existing `handle_payment_result` handles it after the worker completes.
2. **Add** `PaymentUICog._send_admin_approval_dm(payment) -> Optional[discord.Message]`:
   - Build DM content (amount, recipient, redacted wallet, intent_id, jump link).
   - `admin = await bot.fetch_user(int(os.getenv('ADMIN_USER_ID')))`.
   - `view = AdminApprovalView(self, payment['payment_id']); bot.add_view(view); await admin.send(content, view=view)`.
   - On `Forbidden` / HTTPException → ERROR log with `payment_id` + `intent_id`, return `None`.
3. **Add** `PaymentUICog._register_pending_admin_approval_views()` called from `cog_load` AFTER the orphan sweep (Step 18) has run:
   - For each writable guild: `db_handler.list_intents_by_status(guild_id, 'awaiting_admin_approval')`.
   - For each intent with `final_payment_id`: `bot.add_view(AdminApprovalView(self, intent['final_payment_id']))`.
   - Because Rev-4 fix #1 guarantees `awaiting_admin_approval` implies `final_payment_id` is set, the defensive filter here should never skip.
4. **Grants:** untouched.

## Phase 5: New Agent Tools + Tool Infrastructure

### Step 11: Auto-skip verified-wallet path in `execute_initiate_payment` (`src/features/admin_chat/tools.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Modify** `execute_initiate_payment` at `:2543-2595`:
   - Verified wallet: DO NOT create an intent in `awaiting_wallet`. Instead, call `cog._gate_fresh_intent_atomic(channel, guild_id, recipient_user_id, amount_sol, source_channel_id, wallet_record, admin_user_id, reason, producer_ref)` (Step 9.2). That helper encapsulates duplicate check + payment create + atomic intent insert + admin DM. No placeholder ever exists.
   - Unverified wallet: unchanged — intent starts in `awaiting_wallet`, wallet prompt posted.
2. **Do not** modify `_start_admin_payment_flow`.

### Step 12: Read-only query tools (`src/features/admin_chat/tools.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** schemas for `query_payment_state`, `query_wallet_state`, `list_recent_payments`.
2. **Add** executors — thin wrappers + redaction + pop-and-warn guard.
3. **Wire** dispatch.

### Step 13: `initiate_batch_payment` tool (`src/features/admin_chat/tools.py`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **Add** schema: `initiate_batch_payment(payments: [...])` 1..20 entries.
2. **Add** executor:
   - Per-element pop-and-warn.
   - Validate every element (`recipient_user_id > 0`, `amount_sol > 0`, no active duplicate). All-or-none rejection.
   - Look up `wallet_record` for each recipient. Partition into VERIFIED and UNVERIFIED lists.
   - **UNVERIFIED branch:** build intent records with `status='awaiting_wallet'` (correctly — these intents genuinely need a wallet). Call `db_handler.create_admin_payment_intents_batch(unverified_records, guild_id)`. On success, post wallet prompt for each.
   - **VERIFIED branch:** DO NOT create batch intents for these. Instead, iterate one-at-a-time and call `cog._gate_fresh_intent_atomic(...)` per entry — each call performs duplicate check + payment create + atomic intent insert + admin DM. This means a verified entry is NOT part of the atomic Supabase batch insert (because it needs a per-entry `payment_requests` row first). The per-entry ordering still preserves crash safety via Step 18's orphan sweep. Document in the step: all-or-none atomicity applies to the VALIDATION phase and to the UNVERIFIED batch insert; VERIFIED entries are each atomic-per-entry but not atomic-across-entries. A mid-batch crash leaves some verified payments as orphans which the sweep cancels, and the admin must re-invoke for those.
   - 16-payment batch → 16 admin DMs (if all verified) OR 16 wallet prompts (if all unverified) OR a mix.
3. **Wire** dispatch.

**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** schema.
2. **Add** executor:
   - Defense-in-depth admin check via injected `admin_user_id`.
   - Validate wallet via `is_valid_solana_address`.
   - `db_handler.upsert_wallet(...)`. `WalletUpdateBlockedError` → return error.
   - **Explicitly DM admin** via `bot.fetch_user(ADMIN_USER_ID).send(...)`, wrapped in try/except for `Forbidden`/`HTTPException` (log ERROR but don't fail the tool).
   - Return a redacted result.
3. **Wire** dispatch.

## Phase 6: Reconciliation, Orphan Sweep, 24h Timeout, Intent Resolution

### Step 18: Startup orphan-payment sweep + reconciliation (`src/features/admin_chat/admin_chat_cog.py` + `src/features/payments/payment_ui_cog.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** a new startup coroutine `_reconcile_admin_chat_orphan_payments()` on `AdminChatCog`. Called from `_ensure_startup_reconciled` BEFORE `_reconcile_active_intents` AND exposed via a new `bot.admin_chat_cog.reconcile_orphan_payments_for_ui_cog()` accessor so `PaymentUICog.cog_load` can await it before calling `_register_pending_confirmation_views`.
2. **Logic:**
   ```
   writable_guild_ids = self._get_reconciliation_guild_ids()
   pending = payment_service.get_pending_confirmation_payments(guild_ids=writable_guild_ids)
   for payment in pending:
       if payment.get('producer') != 'admin_chat' or payment.get('is_test'):
           continue
       metadata = payment.get('metadata') or {}
       intent_id = metadata.get('intent_id')
       intent = db_handler.get_admin_payment_intent(intent_id, payment['guild_id']) if intent_id else None
       if intent is None:
           # Fallback: find by payment id in case metadata is stale or missing.
           intent = db_handler.find_admin_chat_intent_by_payment_id(payment['payment_id'])
       if intent is None:
           # AUTO-SKIP CRASH: orphan payment, no intent. Cancel the orphan.
           db_handler.cancel_payment(payment['payment_id'], guild_id=payment['guild_id'],
                                     reason='orphan admin_chat real payment on startup — no intent')
           logger.error("[AdminChat] Cancelled orphan admin_chat real payment %s (no intent)", payment['payment_id'])
           continue
       intent_status = (intent.get('status') or '').lower()
       if intent_status == 'awaiting_admin_approval' and intent.get('final_payment_id') == payment['payment_id']:
           # Already wired — _register_pending_admin_approval_views handles it.
           continue
       if intent_status == 'awaiting_confirmation':
           # LEGACY in-flight row — leave for PaymentConfirmView re-registration.
           continue
       if intent_status == 'awaiting_test_receipt_confirmation':
           # TEST-RECEIPT CRASH WINDOW: the positive ack created the payment but died before
           # the intent update. Complete the update and fire the admin DM.
           updated = db_handler.update_admin_payment_intent(intent['intent_id'], {
               'status': 'awaiting_admin_approval',
               'final_payment_id': payment['payment_id'],
           }, intent['guild_id'])
           if updated:
               await bot.payment_ui_cog._send_admin_approval_dm(payment)
               logger.info("[AdminChat] Recovered test-receipt crash for intent %s", intent['intent_id'])
           continue
       # Any other intent status with a stray pending_confirmation payment is anomalous.
       # Fail-closed: cancel the orphan and leave the intent alone for ops review.
       db_handler.cancel_payment(payment['payment_id'], guild_id=payment['guild_id'],
                                 reason=f'orphan admin_chat real payment on startup — intent in {intent_status}')
       logger.error("[AdminChat] Cancelled anomalous admin_chat real payment %s (intent %s in %s)",
                    payment['payment_id'], intent['intent_id'], intent_status)
   ```
3. **Modify** `PaymentUICog.cog_load` so it awaits `bot.admin_chat_cog.reconcile_orphan_payments_for_ui_cog()` BEFORE calling `_register_pending_confirmation_views` and `_register_pending_admin_approval_views`. Ordering guarantee: orphans are resolved before any view decision is made.
4. **Extend** the existing `_reconcile_active_intents` walker:
   - `awaiting_test_receipt_confirmation` → `_reconcile_intent_history` + replay via `_handle_pending_recipient_message`.
   - `awaiting_admin_approval` → log count.
   - `manual_review` → log count.
5. **Modify** `_reconcile_intent_history` to call `_handle_pending_recipient_message(message, refreshed_intent)` per recipient message.
6. **Delete** `_classify_payment_reply` and `_check_pending_payment_reply` once no callers remain (grep-verify).

### Step 19: 24h timeout sweep (`src/features/admin_chat/admin_chat_cog.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `@tasks.loop(minutes=15) _sweep_stale_test_receipts`.
2. **Logic:** scan `list_stale_test_receipt_intents(cutoff)`; transition to `manual_review`, post admin tag, DM admin. Per-intent try/except.
3. **Start** in `cog_load`; stop in `cog_unload`.

### Step 20: `resolve_admin_intent` with cascade cancellation (`src/features/admin_chat/tools.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** schema: `resolve_admin_intent(intent_id: str, note?: str)`. No `resolution` parameter — the tool always writes `cancelled`.
2. **Add** executor:
   - Require trusted `admin_user_id`; verify vs `ADMIN_USER_ID`.
   - Fetch intent; refuse already-terminal statuses (`completed`/`failed`/`cancelled`).
   - **Cascade-cancel linked `payment_requests` rows BEFORE updating the intent** (closes FLAG-014, correctness-2, scope-2, all_locations-1, callers-2):
     ```python
     for payment_field in ('final_payment_id', 'test_payment_id'):
         linked_payment_id = intent.get(payment_field)
         if not linked_payment_id:
             continue
         linked_row = db_handler.get_payment_request(linked_payment_id, guild_id=guild_id)
         if linked_row and linked_row.get('status') in ('pending_confirmation', 'queued'):
             try:
                 db_handler.cancel_payment(
                     linked_payment_id,
                     guild_id=guild_id,
                     reason=f'resolve_admin_intent cascade: {note or "(no note)"}',
                 )
             except Exception as e:
                 logger.error("[AdminChat] Cascade cancel failed for %s: %s", linked_payment_id, e)
                 return {"success": False, "error": f"Cascade cancel failed for {payment_field}={linked_payment_id}: {e}"}
         elif linked_row and linked_row.get('status') in ('submitted', 'confirmed', 'manual_hold'):
             # Refuse to resolve intents whose payment is mid-flight or already confirmed —
             # operator must use /payment-resolve first.
             return {"success": False, "error": f"{payment_field}={linked_payment_id} is in non-cancellable status '{linked_row['status']}'; use /payment-resolve first"}
     ```
     Mirrors the existing decline handler at `admin_chat_cog.py:616-620` which cancels `final_payment_id` before marking the intent cancelled.
   - After cascade succeeds: `db_handler.update_admin_payment_intent(intent_id, {'status': 'cancelled'}, guild_id)`.
   - Explicit admin DM (same pattern as Step 14).
3. **Wire** dispatch. Registered via Step 15 and Step 16.

## Phase 7: Tests + Verification

### Step 21: Rewrite `tests/test_admin_payments.py`
**Scope:** Large
1. **Remove** tests mocking `_classify_payment_reply`.
2. **Parser unit tests** (punctuation-wrapped wallet, keyword parametrization).
3. **Identity router tests** (admin plain-channel, pending-recipient with Anthropic mock raising, fallthrough, approved-member no-route).
4. **State-machine tests** (awaiting_wallet valid/ignore, test-receipt positive/negative/ambiguous).
5. **Payment-gate tests:**
   - `test_producer_flows_admin_chat_real_authorizes_admin_dm`.
   - `test_admin_approval_view_only_admin_can_click`.
   - `test_admin_approval_view_persists_across_restart`.
   - `test_admin_approval_click_queues_payment_and_posts_queued_message`.
   - `test_admin_dm_fail_closed_on_forbidden`.
   - `test_payment_confirm_view_not_sent_for_admin_chat_real_payments`.
   - `test_restart_filter_state_aware` — (a) admin_chat + `awaiting_admin_approval` intent → `PaymentConfirmView` NOT re-registered; (b) admin_chat + legacy `awaiting_confirmation` intent → re-registered; (c) grants → re-registered.
6. **Auto-skip + batch tests:**
   - `test_auto_skip_verified_wallet_creates_intent_in_awaiting_admin_approval_atomically` — assert the intent is INSERTED with `status='awaiting_admin_approval'` AND `final_payment_id` set in the same create call. No intermediate status exists at any point.
   - `test_auto_skip_no_placeholder_awaiting_wallet_intent` — assert NO intent is ever observed in `awaiting_wallet` during the verified-wallet path.
   - `test_initiate_batch_payment_atomic_verified_and_unverified_partitions` — invalid element → zero writes; valid all-verified → N payment_requests rows + N intents atomic per-entry + N admin DMs; valid all-unverified → single atomic batch insert + N wallet prompts; mixed → unverified batch atomic + verified per-entry atomic.
7. **Orphan sweep tests (Step 18):**
   - `test_orphan_sweep_auto_skip_crash_cancels_payment` — create a `pending_confirmation` admin_chat real payment with no matching intent; run sweep; assert `cancel_payment` was called and the payment is cancelled.
   - `test_orphan_sweep_test_receipt_crash_completes_recovery` — create a `pending_confirmation` admin_chat real payment whose metadata points at an intent still in `awaiting_test_receipt_confirmation`; run sweep; assert the intent is transitioned to `awaiting_admin_approval` with `final_payment_id` set AND `_send_admin_approval_dm` is called.
   - `test_orphan_sweep_leaves_legacy_awaiting_confirmation_alone` — admin_chat real payment with intent in `awaiting_confirmation` → sweep does nothing; `_register_pending_confirmation_views` re-registers the `PaymentConfirmView`.
   - `test_orphan_sweep_leaves_already_wired_intent_alone` — admin_chat real payment with intent in `awaiting_admin_approval` + matching `final_payment_id` → sweep skips; `_register_pending_admin_approval_views` re-registers the view.
   - `test_orphan_sweep_runs_before_view_registration` — assert call order in `PaymentUICog.cog_load`: orphan sweep → then `_register_pending_confirmation_views` → then `_register_pending_admin_approval_views`.
8. **New-tool tests:** upsert admin-only + explicit admin DM (mock `bot.fetch_user(...).send`), query tool safety, `WalletUpdateBlockedError` path.
9. **Lifecycle + resolve tests:**
   - `test_manual_review_blocks_new_intents`.
   - `test_resolve_admin_intent_cascade_cancels_final_payment` — intent has `final_payment_id` in `pending_confirmation`; call `resolve_admin_intent`; assert `cancel_payment` was called for the linked payment AND the intent transitions to `cancelled`.
   - `test_resolve_admin_intent_cascade_cancels_test_payment` — same with `test_payment_id`.
   - `test_resolve_admin_intent_refuses_non_cancellable_linked_payment` — linked payment is `submitted` or `confirmed`; assert the tool returns an error and the intent is NOT cancelled.
   - `test_resolve_admin_intent_admin_injection` — non-admin caller rejected.
   - `test_resolve_admin_intent_rejects_completed_schema_parameter` — no `resolution` param accepted.
   - `test_24h_timeout_sweep_escalates_stuck_test_receipt`.
10. **Infrastructure tests:** `ADMIN_ONLY_TOOLS` and `_ADMIN_IDENTITY_INJECTED_TOOLS` exhaustive checks.

## Execution Order
1. **Phase 1** (schema + DB helpers incl. `find_admin_chat_intent_by_payment_id`, `get_pending_confirmation_admin_chat_intents_by_payment`).
2. **Phase 2** (producer_flows + restart-recovery filter).
3. **Phase 3** (parsers + identity router).
4. **Phase 4** (flow rewiring + `_gate_existing_intent` + `_gate_fresh_intent_atomic` + `AdminApprovalView`).
5. **Phase 5** (agent tools + infrastructure).
6. **Phase 6** (orphan sweep FIRST, then existing reconciliation, then 24h sweep, then `resolve_admin_intent` cascade).
7. **Phase 7** (tests + verification).

13. Invariant suites.
14. Full `pytest`.
15. Manual smoke including two crash-window scenarios.

  "assumptions": [
    "Verified-wallet fast path: the intent row is INSERTED (not updated) with `status='awaiting_admin_approval'` and `final_payment_id` set in ONE atomic `create_admin_payment_intent` call. No prior placeholder intent exists at any point. The DB unique partial index on (guild_id, channel_id, recipient_user_id) WHERE status NOT IN (terminal) provides atomicity against concurrent rapid-fire initiations.",
    "`create_admin_payment_intent` at `src/common/db_handler.py:1805-1819` accepts arbitrary fields in the record dict (it passes them verbatim to Supabase `.insert`). Both `status` and `final_payment_id` can be set in the initial insert. If this assumption fails, the helper can generate `intent_id` client-side and use a single `.upsert` instead.",
    "The `metadata.intent_id` field on `payment_requests` is the canonical link from payment \u2192 intent. For the fresh-intent fast path, the client generates `intent_id` via `uuid4()` BEFORE calling `request_payment` and passes it in metadata AND uses it as the intent primary key.",
    "Startup orphan sweep runs in `AdminChatCog._ensure_startup_reconciled` and is awaited by `PaymentUICog.cog_load` via a new `bot.admin_chat_cog.reconcile_orphan_payments_for_ui_cog()` accessor. Ordering guarantee: sweep completes before ANY `PaymentConfirmView` or `AdminApprovalView` is registered.",
    "The orphan sweep cancels orphans via the existing `db_handler.cancel_payment(payment_id, guild_id)` primitive at `src/common/db_handler.py:2402-2420` \u2014 no new cancellation path is introduced.",
    "`resolve_admin_intent` cascade refuses to act if any linked payment is in non-cancellable status (`submitted`, `confirmed`, `manual_hold`). Operator must use `/payment-resolve` first to reconcile such payments.",
    "`initiate_batch_payment` splits verified and unverified partitions. UNVERIFIED entries use the atomic batch insert (true all-or-none). VERIFIED entries iterate per-entry through `_gate_fresh_intent_atomic`, which is atomic per-entry but not atomic-across-entries; a mid-batch crash leaves some verified payments as orphans that the sweep cancels on restart. This is a documented trade-off.",
    "`manual_review` remains a BLOCKING state. `has_active_payment_or_intent`, `get_active_intent_for_recipient`, `list_active_intents`, and the unique partial index all treat it as active. Admin clears via `resolve_admin_intent(intent_id)` which cascade-cancels any linked payment then marks the intent cancelled.",
    "`receipt_prompt_message_id` is a new column distinct from `prompt_message_id`; safe-delete clears both.",
    "`PaymentActorKind.ADMIN_DM` is reused; no new enum value.",
    "`PRODUCER_FLOWS['admin_chat'].real_confirmed_by` is extended to `{RECIPIENT_CLICK, RECIPIENT_MESSAGE, ADMIN_DM}`. RECIPIENT_* stay as safety-belt for legacy rows.",
    "`send_confirmation_request` is unchanged; gating happens at the removed call site (`admin_chat_cog.py:393`) and via the state-aware restart filter (Step 4) + the orphan sweep (Step 18).",
    "`MEMBER_SYSTEM_PROMPT`, `_can_user_message_bot`, `_is_directed_at_bot` stay as dead code.",
    "Batch tool accepts 1..20 payments per call.",
    "24h timeout sweep at 15-minute intervals in `AdminChatCog`.",
    "AdminApprovalView click handler posts 'queued for sending'; the existing `handle_payment_result` emits the 'Payment sent.' message after worker completion.",
    "`upsert_wallet_for_user` executor explicitly DMs the admin via `bot.fetch_user(ADMIN_USER_ID).send(...)` wrapped in try/except.",
    "Wallet parser uses tolerant delimiter regex + `is_valid_solana_address`.",
    "Query tools apply existing redaction helpers."
  ],

For each check, investigate the codebase, then add your findings to the `findings` array for that check.

Each finding needs:
- "detail": what you specifically checked and what you found (at least a full sentence)
- "flagged": true if this describes a difference, risk, or tension — even if you think it's justified. false only if purely informational with no possible downside.
- Every check must end with at least one finding. Never leave a `findings` array empty. If you found no issue, add one detailed `flagged: false` finding explaining what you checked and why it appears clear.

When in doubt, flag it — the gate can accept tradeoffs, but it can't act on findings it never sees.

Bad: {"detail": "The hints suggest approach X but the plan uses Y. However Y is consistent with X's intent.", "flagged": false}  ← a different approach than the hints IS a flag. You found a divergence — flag it. The gate decides if it's acceptable.

After filling in checks, add any additional concerns to the `flags` array (e.g., security, performance, dependencies).
Use the standard format (id, concern, category, severity_hint, evidence). This array can be empty.

Workflow: read the file → investigate → read file again → add finding → write file back. Repeat for each check.

This is critique iteration 4. The template file includes prior findings with their status. Verify addressed flags were actually fixed, re-flag if inadequate, and check for new issues introduced by the revision.

        Additional guidelines:
        - Robustness level: standard. Use balanced judgment. Flag significant risks, but do not spend flags on minor polish or executor-obvious boilerplate.

        - Reuse existing flag IDs when the same concern is still open.
        - `verified_flag_ids`: list flag IDs from prior iterations that the revised plan actually resolves (e.g., if the plan was revised to fix FLAG-001, and you confirm the fix is correct, include "FLAG-001"). Only include flags you've verified — don't guess.
        - Verify that the plan follows the expected structure when validator warnings or the outline suggest drift.
        - Additional flags may use these categories: correctness, security, completeness, performance, maintainability, other.

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/banodoco-workspace/brain-of-bndc

        Engineering brief produced from the codebase and task details:

        ### Task Summary
        Refactor admin_chat to route messages by author identity (admin→agent, pending-recipient→state machine, else→passthrough), add a new `awaiting_test_receipt_confirmation` intent state with keyword-based confirm/negative/ambiguous parsing, add query_*/initiate_batch_payment/upsert_wallet_for_user agent tools, introduce an admin-DM-only AdminApprovalView approval gate for real admin_chat payments (replacing PaymentConfirmView for that producer only), auto-skip the test phase for verified wallets, and add a 24h timeout escalation. Grants flow and all hardening/cleanup/polish invariants stay intact.

        ### Key Evidence
        - Today admin_chat routing is content-based: on_message first calls _check_pending_payment_reply which intercepts any recipient reply in any channel for any active intent, then falls through to mention/DM detection and agent invocation. Identity routing replaces both branches with a single author-id switch. (source: src/features/admin_chat/admin_chat_cog.py:790-835 (on_message), :563-640 (_check_pending_payment_reply); relevance: high)
- Current intent status CHECK constraint is ('awaiting_wallet','awaiting_test','awaiting_confirmation','confirmed','completed','failed','cancelled'). No 'awaiting_test_receipt_confirmation' exists; a SQL migration must add it to the CHECK constraint. (source: sql/admin_payment_intents.sql:28-37; relevance: high)
- Today's flow creates a real test payment and AUTO-confirms it via PaymentActorKind.AUTO in _start_admin_payment_flow, then on terminal test success creates the real payment and calls PaymentUICog.send_confirmation_request for recipient confirmation. The new flow must instead park the intent in awaiting_test_receipt_confirmation after the test confirms on-chain, then wait for the recipient's text ack before creating the real payment. (source: src/features/admin_chat/admin_chat_cog.py:234-309 (_start_admin_payment_flow, _handle_confirmation_received) and :311-414 (handle_payment_result test branch); relevance: high)
- PaymentConfirmView is used for BOTH grants and admin_chat real payments today — both grants_cog.py:719 and admin_chat_cog.py:393 call payment_ui_cog.send_confirmation_request. The refactor must NOT drop the grants call site, only the admin_chat call site. (source: src/features/grants/grants_cog.py:719, src/features/admin_chat/admin_chat_cog.py:393; relevance: high)
- Reply classification today uses a Claude-based _classify_payment_reply call per message; the new pending-recipient state machine must be deterministic with zero LLM calls. The task mandates hardcoded regex/keyword matching for both wallet detection and confirmation keywords. (source: src/features/admin_chat/admin_chat_cog.py:64 (_classify_payment_reply) and :585-636 branches; relevance: high)
- is_valid_solana_address already exists and does base58 decode + 32-byte length check — reuse it rather than re-implementing. It's imported in admin_chat_cog and used at :586,:592. (source: src/features/grants/solana_client.py:31-39 (is_valid_solana_address); relevance: high)
- execute_initiate_payment already has wallet-verification auto-skip logic: it filters wallet_record by verified_at and only calls _start_admin_payment_flow when a verified wallet is present. However, _start_admin_payment_flow currently issues the TEST payment unconditionally — it never 'goes straight to real'. The auto-skip must be implemented by branching at intent creation time, not inside _start_admin_payment_flow. (source: src/features/admin_chat/tools.py:2543-2574, admin_chat_cog.py:234-284; relevance: high)
- DB layer is Supabase REST — there is no generic multi-row transaction helper on DatabaseHandler. create_admin_payment_intent inserts one row per call. Atomic batch creation (initiate_batch_payment) will require either (a) a new Postgres RPC function wrapping N inserts in a txn, or (b) a new db_handler.create_admin_payment_intents_batch method that uses a single Supabase insert([...]) call plus an explicit rollback path on partial failure. Supabase .insert([list]) is atomic per-request on PostgREST but only within one table — sufficient here since all rows are admin_payment_intents. (source: src/common/db_handler.py:1805-1819 (create_admin_payment_intent); relevance: high)
- PaymentUICog persistent-view registration pattern: cog_load → _register_pending_confirmation_views → bot.add_view(PaymentConfirmView(self, payment_id)) for every pending payment. The new AdminApprovalView must follow the same shape, keyed by payment_id (not intent_id) with custom_id='payment_admin_approve:{payment_id}' so restart recovery works. (source: src/features/payments/payment_ui_cog.py:19-93, :110-130; relevance: high)
- ADMIN_USER_ID is an env var read in admin_chat_cog.py:50 (cached as self.admin_user_id) AND separately in payment_ui_cog.py:165. The AdminApprovalView button callback must independently re-check the interacting user matches ADMIN_USER_ID (defense-in-depth — custom_id can't be spoofed but discord.py permissions model is view-level). (source: src/features/admin_chat/admin_chat_cog.py:50, src/features/payments/payment_ui_cog.py:165; relevance: high)
- safe_delete_messages lives in src/common/discord_utils.py:350-451 with signature (channel, message_ids, *, logger) returning DeleteCounts. Already used by payment_worker_cog.py:172. Reuse for 'delete stale flow messages in the thread' after approval. (source: src/common/discord_utils.py:350-451; relevance: medium)
- Startup reconciliation loop in admin_chat_cog only walks {awaiting_wallet, awaiting_confirmation, awaiting_test, confirmed} branches. Must be extended to recognize 'awaiting_test_receipt_confirmation' and the new 'awaiting_admin_approval' state, and to fire the 24h timeout sweep. (source: src/features/admin_chat/admin_chat_cog.py:546-561 (_reconcile_active_intents); relevance: high)
- There is NO existing 24-hour timeout loop — only a 30s payment worker loop (payment_worker_cog.py:92) and startup reconciliation. A new background task (either in AdminChatCog or a dedicated IntentTimeoutCog) must be added to scan awaiting_test_receipt_confirmation rows older than 24h and escalate. (source: src/features/payments/payment_worker_cog.py:92; relevance: high)
- Wallet upsert today goes through db_handler.upsert_wallet with a WalletUpdateBlockedError guard when an active payment intent exists for the user. The new upsert_wallet_for_user tool must respect this guard (the admin must see the error), and must NOT set verified_at — that only happens after a successful test round-trip via existing mark_wallet_verified at payment_service.py:910,967. (source: src/features/admin_chat/admin_chat_cog.py:201-215, src/features/payments/payment_service.py:910,967, src/common/db_handler.py:1649 (mark_wallet_verified); relevance: high)
- Current agent initiate_payment tool definition at tools.py:580-595 takes (recipient_user_id, amount_sol, reason?). The new initiate_batch_payment tool takes a payments[] array; per the task, batches MUST be implemented so that per-payment mechanics (auto-skip, test cycle, admin DM) fan out individually — a batch of 16 produces 16 DMs, not one. This implies batch is really 'create N intents atomically then let each flow through the existing per-intent pipeline'. (source: src/features/admin_chat/tools.py:580-595 (tool schema), :2494-2595 (executor); relevance: high)
- The agent has no read-only payment/wallet state tools today. It has list_payments/list_wallets/get_payment_status (write-admin tools under 'Doing things' in the system prompt) but these are generic ledger inspectors. The task asks for three NEW narrowly-scoped tools (query_payment_state, query_wallet_state, list_recent_payments) — these are net-new, not renames of existing tools. Keep existing list_payments/list_wallets/get_payment_status in place; add the new three alongside. (source: src/features/admin_chat/agent.py:74-77 (list_wallets, list_payments, get_payment_status); relevance: medium)
- Under identity-based routing, approved-member messages (the _can_user_message_bot path at admin_chat_cog.py:694-714) stop reaching the agent unless the author is ADMIN_USER_ID — an intentional scoping reduction. MEMBER_SYSTEM_PROMPT (agent.py:151) becomes effectively dead code; do not delete it, but note in the plan that the member branch is disabled at the cog level. (source: src/features/admin_chat/admin_chat_cog.py:808-817, src/features/admin_chat/agent.py:151; relevance: medium)
- Reconciliation at startup walks discord_messages and re-invokes _check_pending_payment_reply per recipient message. After the refactor, _check_pending_payment_reply is replaced by the identity router's pending-recipient branch, but the startup reconciler must still be able to invoke it synthetically to catch up on missed messages during downtime. (source: src/features/admin_chat/admin_chat_cog.py:498-544 (_reconcile_intent_history); relevance: medium)
- handle_payment_result currently creates the final payment inline in the test-success branch. After the refactor, test-success no longer creates the final payment — it transitions the intent to awaiting_test_receipt_confirmation and waits for the recipient's text. The real payment is created only when the state machine processes a positive confirmation, and then transitions to awaiting_admin_approval (new state) which triggers the admin DM. (source: src/features/admin_chat/admin_chat_cog.py:311-414 (handle_payment_result); relevance: high)
- Existing test file test_admin_payments.py (1350 lines) has dense coverage of the current flow: wallet classification via Claude, WalletUpdateBlockedError, auto-confirm of test payments, final payment flow. Many tests will need updating because the state machine transitions and reply handling change fundamentally. Flag as 'expect broad test churn in test_admin_payments.py'. (source: tests/test_admin_payments.py:1-1350 (full file); relevance: high)
- Hardening invariants to preserve: per-user-per-day caps, canonical wallet swap defense, idempotency collision detection, WalletUpdateBlockedError, RLS, prompt-injection hardening (wallet lookups only from wallet_registry — NEVER from LLM-provided strings), null-recipient fail-closed. The new batch tool and upsert tool must also never accept an LLM-sourced wallet address without going through wallet_registry/upsert_wallet — the existing 'ignore injected wallet_address' pattern at tools.py:2501-2508 is the template. (source: src/features/admin_chat/tools.py:2501-2508 (wallet injection guard pattern); relevance: high)

- [fail_to_pass] tests/test_admin_payments.py::test_state_machine_awaiting_wallet_ignores_non_address: awaiting_wallet + non-address text → no action, no reply, intent stays in awaiting_wallet (no spam).

- [fail_to_pass] tests/test_admin_payments.py::test_auto_skip_verified_wallet_creates_real_payment_directly: initiate_payment with recipient having wallet_registry.verified_at IS NOT NULL skips the test payment entirely — no is_test=True row is created, a real payment row goes to awaiting_admin_approval.
